'Suvatthi' (well-being) is in the sense of a blessing. In blame, 'dhī' (fie!) is said. For 'where': kuhiñcanaṃ, kuhiṃ, kutra, kuttha, kattha, kahaṃ, kva, tha.

1161.

1161.

Ihe’dhā’tra [M 0.0095] tu etthā’ttha,

Atha sabbatra sabbadhi;

Kadā kudācanaṃ cātha, tadāni ca tadā samā.

For 'here, in this place': iha, idha, atra, ettha, attha. For 'everywhere': sabbatra, sabbadhi. For 'when, at any time': kadā, kudācanaṃ. For 'then, at that time': tadāni, tadā. (These are similar).

1162.

1162.

Ādikamme bhusatthe ca, sambhavo’tiṇṇa tittisu;

Niyogi’ssariya’ppīti, dāna pūjā’gga, santisu.

The prefix 'pa-' is used in the senses of: initial action, abundance; arising, excessive satisfaction; appointment, lordship, delight; giving, veneration, excellence, and peace.

1163.

1163.

Dassane tappare saṅge, pasaṃsā, gati, suddhisu;

Hiṃsā, pakāranto’bhāva, viyogā’vayavesu ca;

Po’pasaggo disāyoge, patthanā, dhitiādisu.

The prefix 'pa-' is also used in the senses of: seeing, devotion, association; praise, destiny, purity; harm, manner, disappearance, separation, components; and in connection with direction, aspiration, resolve, and so on.

1164.

1164.

Parāsaddo parihāni, parājaya gatīsu ca;

Bhusatthe paṭilomatthe, vikkamā’masanādisu.

The word 'parā' is used in the senses of: decline, defeat, and movements; abundance and opposition; effort, touching, and so on.

1165.

1165.

Nissesā’bhāva sanyāsa, bhusattha mokkha rāsisu;

Gehā’deso’pamāhīna, pasādaniggatā’ccaye.

The prefix 'ni-' or 'nir-' is used for: completeness, absence, renunciation; abundance, liberation, heaps; household matters, instruction, comparison, inferiority; confidence, emergence, and transgression.

1166.

1166.

Dassano’sānanikkhantā, dhobhāgesva’vadhāraṇe;

Sāmīpye bandhane cheka, ntobhāgo’paratīsu ca.

The prefix 'ni-' or 'nir-' is also used for: seeing, conclusion, emergence; lower parts, ascertainment; nearness, binding, cleverness; inner parts, and cessation.

1167.

1167.

Pātubhāve viyoge ca, nisedhādo ni dissati;

Atho nīharaṇe cevā, varaṇādo ca nī siyā.

In manifestation, separation, negation, and so on, 'ni' is seen. And also in taking out, prevention, and so on, 'nī' may be used.

1168.

1168.

Uddhakamma viyoga tta, lābha titti samiddhisu;

Pātubhāva’ccayābhāva, pabalatte pakāsane;

Dakkha’ggatāsu kathane, sattimokkhādike u ca.

The prefix 'u-' or 'ud-' is used for: upward action, separation, attainment; gain, satisfaction, success; manifestation, decline, absence; strength, declaration; skill, excellence, speaking; power, liberation, and so on.

1169.

1169.

Du kucchite’sadatthesu, virūpatte pya’sobhane;

Siyā’bhāvā’samiddhīsu, kicche cā’nandanādike.

The prefix 'du-' is used for: the vile, worthless things; ugliness, disgrace; absence, lack of success; difficulty, unpleasantness, and so on.

1170.

1170.

Saṃ samodhāna saṅkhepa, samantatta samiddhisu;

Sammā bhusa saha ppatthā, bhimukhatthesu saṅgate;

Vidhāne pabhave pūjā, punappunakriyādisu.

The prefix 'saṃ-' is used for: union, condensation, entirety, success; rightness, abundance, togetherness, aspiration; the sense of facing, concurrence; disposition, power, homage, repeated action, and so on.

1171.

1171.

Vividhā’tisayā’bhāva, bhusatti’ssariyā’ccaye;

Viyoge kalahe pātu, bhāve bhāse ca kucchane.

The prefix 'vi-' is used for: variousness, excess, absence; abundance, lordship, destruction; separation, conflict, manifestation; speech, and blaming.

1172.

1172.

Dūrā’nabhimukhattesu, mohā’navaṭṭhitīsu ca;

Padhāna dakkhatā kheda, sahatthādo vi dissati.

The prefix 'vi-' is also used for: remoteness, averted faces; delusion, instability; striving, skill, weariness, self-application, and so on, 'vi' is seen.

1173.

1173.

Viyoge [M 0.0096] jānane cā’dho,bhāga nicchaya bhāga’nicchaya (bhāga+anicchaya-ṭī) suddhisu;

Īsadatthe paribhave, desa byāpana hānisu;

Vacokriyāya theyye ca, ñāṇappattādike ava.

The prefix 'ava-' is used for: separation, discernment; inferiority, part, ascertainment, purity; the sense of 'a little', disregard; location, pervasion, detriment; the act of speech, thievery; attainment of knowledge, and so on.

1174.

1174.

Pacchā bhusatta sādisyā, nupacchinnā’nuvattisu;

Hīne ca tatiyatthā’dho, bhāgesva’nugate hite;

Dese lakkhaṇa vicche’ttha, mbhūta bhāgādike anu.

The prefix 'anu-' is used for: afterward, abundance, similarity; uninterrupted continuation; inferiority, the sense of the third, lower parts; following, welfare; place, characteristic, repetition, specific being, parts, and so on.

1175.

1175.

Samantatthe paricchede, pūjā’liṅgana vajjane;

Dosakkhāne nivāsanā, vaññā’dhāresu bhojane;

Soka byāpana tatvesu, lakkhaṇādo siyā pari.

The prefix 'pari-' may be used for: completeness, limitation; worship, embracing, avoidance; fault-finding, dwelling, disregard, bases, food; sorrow, pervasion, realities, characteristics, and so on.

1176.

1176.

Ābhimukhya visiṭṭhu’ddha, kammasāruppavuddhisu;

Pūjā’dhika kulā’sacca, lakkhaṇādimhi cāpya’bhi.

The prefix 'abhi-' is used for: facing, excellence, superiority; suitable action, growth; worship, excess, family, truth, characteristics, and so on.

1177.

1177.

Adhiki’ssara, pāṭhā’dhi, ṭṭhāna, pāpuṇanesva’dhi;

Nicchaye coparittā’dhi, bhavane ca visesane.

The prefix 'adhi-' is used for: superiority, lordship, reading, determination, attainment; ascertainment, being above, overcoming, and distinction.

1178.

1178.

Paṭidānanisedhesu, vāmā’dānanivattisu;

Sādise paṭinidhimhi, ābhimukhyagatīsu ca.

For 'paṭi-': in giving back, prohibitions; in turning back from wrong taking; in likeness, substitution; in movements facing forward.

1179.

1179.

Patibodhe patigate, tathā punakriyāya ca;

Sambhāvane paṭiccatthe, patīti lakkhaṇādike;

Su sobhane sukhe sammā, bhusa suṭṭhu samiddhisu.

For 'paṭi-': in understanding, reaching; in repeated action; in probability, reciprocal meaning, conviction, characteristics, and so on. For 'su-': in beauty, happiness, rightness, abundance, excellence, complete success.

1180.

1180.

Ābhimukhya, samīpā’di, kammā’liṅgana pattisu;

Mariyādu’ddhakammi’cchā, bandhanā’bhividhīsu ā.

For 'ā-': in facing, proximity, etc.; in action, embracing, attainment; in limit, upward action, desire; in binding, methods.

1181.

1181.

Nivāsā’vhāna gahaṇa, kicche’sattha nivattisu;

Appasādā’si saraṇa, patiṭṭhā’vimhayādisu.

For 'ni-': in dwelling, invitation, grasping; in difficulty, worthlessness, turning back; in displeasure, refuge, foundation, wonder, and so on.

1182.

1182.

Antobhāva bhusattā’ti, saya pūjāsva’tikkame;

Bhūtabhāve pasaṃsāyaṃ, daḷhatthādo siyā ati.

For 'ati-': in inclusion, abundance, excess; in self-worship, transgression; in true state, praise; in the sense of firmness, and so on, 'ati' may occur.

1183.

1183.

Sambhāvane ca garahā, pekkhāsu ca samuccaye;

Pañhe saṃvaraṇe ceva, āsīsatthe apī’ritaṃ.

For 'api': in estimation, censure, expectation, aggregation; in question, restraint; in the sense of blessing, 'api' is used.

1184.

1184.

Niddese vajjane pūjā, pagate vāraṇepi ca;

Padussane ca garahā, corikā’do siyā apa.

For 'apa-': in explanation, rejection, veneration; in departure, prevention; in offense, censure; in theft, and so on, 'apa' may occur.

1185.

1185.

Samīpapūjā [M 0.0097] sādissa, dosakkhāno’papattisu;

Bhusatto’pagamā’dhikya, pubbakammanivattisu;

Gayhākāro’parittesu, upe’tya upetya=upa+iti’nasanā’dike.

For 'upa-': in nearness, worship, similarity; in fault-finding, occurrence; in abundance, approach, excess; in turning back from former deeds; in grasping, manner, insignificance; in approaching, fasting, and so on.

1186.

1186.

Evaṃ nidassanā’kāro, pamāsu sampahaṃsane;

Upadese ca vacana, paṭiggāhe’vadhāraṇe;

Garahāye’damatthe ca, parimāne ca pucchane.

For 'evaṃ': in illustration, manner; in measures, delight; in instruction, word, acceptance, ascertainment; in censure, purpose, quantity, and questioning.

1187.

1187.

Samuccaye samāhāre, nvācaye ce’tarītare;

Padapūraṇamatte ca, casaddo avadhāraṇe.

For 'ca': in aggregation, accumulation, concomitance, alternation; merely for filling the verse; and the word 'ca' is used for ascertainment.

1188.

1188.

Iti hetupakāresu, ādimhi cā’vadhāraṇe;

Nidassane padatthassa, vipallāse samāpane.

For 'iti': in cause, modes; in the beginning, ascertainment; in illustration of the meaning of words, reversal, and conclusion.

1189.

1189.

Samuccaye co’pamāyaṃ, saṃsaye padapūraṇe;

Vavatthitavibhāsāyaṃ, vā’vassagge vikappane.

For 'vā': in aggregation, comparison, doubt, filling the verse; in definitive option, abandonment, and alternation.

1190.

1190.

Bhūsane vāraṇe cā’laṃ, vuccate pariyattiyaṃ;

Atho’thā’nantarā’rambha, pañhesu padapūraṇe.

For 'alaṃ': in ornamentation, warding off, and sufficiency, 'alaṃ' is said. For 'atha' and 'atho': in immediate commencement, questions, and filling the verse.

1191.

1191.

Pasaṃsāgarahāsaññā, sīkārādo svīkāra (ṭī.) pi nāma tha;

Nicchaye cā’numānasmiṃ, siyā nūna vitakkane.

For 'nāma': in praise, censure, recognition, acceptance, and so on, 'nāma' is used. For 'nūna': in ascertainment, inference, and reasoning, 'nūna' may occur.

1192.

1192.

Pucchā’vadhāraṇā’nuññā, sāntvanā’lapane santanālapane (ka.) nanu;

Vate’kaṃsa, dayā, hāsa, khedā’lapana, vimhaye.

For 'nanu': in questioning, ascertainment, permission, comforting, and addressing. For 'vata': in certainty, pity, laughter, sorrow, addressing, and wonder.

1193.

1193.

Vākyārambha, visādesu, handa hāse’nukampane;

Yāva tu tāva sākalya, mānā’vadhya’vadhāraṇe.

For 'handa': in the beginning of speech, despair, laughter, and compassion. For 'yāva' and 'tāva': in completeness, measure, limit, and ascertainment.

1194.

1194.

Pācī, pura, ṅgatotthesu, puratthā paṭhame pyatha;

Pabandhe ca cirātīte, nikaṭāgāmike purā.

For 'purā' and related terms: in the east, before, in front; in the first. Also in connection, the distant past, and the near future, 'purā' is used.

1195.

1195.

Nisedhavākyālaṅkārā, vadhāraṇapasiddhisu;

Khalvā’sanne tu abhito-

Bhimukho’bhayatodike.

For 'khalu': in ornamentation of prohibitive sentences, ascertainment, and establishment. For 'abhito': in nearness, facing, on both sides, and so on.

1196.

1196.

Kāmaṃ yadyapisaddatthe, ekaṃsatthe ca dissati;

Atho pana visesasmiṃ, tatheva padapūraṇe.

For 'kāmaṃ': in the sense of 'although' and in the sense of certainty, it is seen. For 'pana': in a specific sense, and likewise for filling the verse.

1197.

1197.

Hi [M 0.0098] kāraṇe visesā’va, dhāraṇe padapūraṇe;

Tu hetuvajje tatthā’tha, ku pāpe’sattha’kucchane.

For 'hi': in cause, distinction, ascertainment, and filling the verse. For 'tu': in cause, exception, there, and then. For 'ku': in evil, worthlessness, and contempt.

1198.

1198.

Nu saṃsaye ca pañhe tha, nānā’ nekattha vajjane;

Kiṃ tu pucchājigucchāsu, kaṃ tu vārimhi muddhani.

For 'nu': in doubt and question. For 'nānā': in various meanings and exclusion. For 'kiṃ': in question and repugnance. For 'kaṃ': in water and on the head.

1199.

1199.

Amā sahasamīpe tha, bhede appaṭhame puna;

Kirā’nussavā’rucisu, udā’pyatthe vikappane.

For 'amā': in haste and proximity. For 'puna': in division, not at first, and again. For 'kira': in tradition and disapproval. For 'udāhu': in the sense of a question and in alternation.

1200.

1200.

Patīcī carime pacchā, sāmi tvaddhe jigucchane;

Pakāse sambhave pātu,

Aññoññe tu raho mitho.

For 'pacchā' and related terms: in the west, in the last, behind. For 'sāmi' and 'addhā': in the sense of half. In disgust. For 'pātu': in manifestation and origin. For 'mitho': mutually and in secret.

1201.

1201.

Hā khedasokadukkhesu, khede tva’haha vimhaye;

Bhiṃsāpane hiṃsāpane (ṭī.) dhī dhi (ka.) nindāyaṃ, pidhāne tiriyaṃ tiro.

For 'hā': in weariness, sorrow, and suffering. For 'ahaha': in weariness and astonishment. For 'dhī': in frightening and blame. For 'tiriyaṃ' and 'tiro': in covering, sideways, and concealment.

1202.

1202.

Tuna tvāna tave tvā tuṃ, dhā so thā kkhattu, meva ca;

To tha tra hiñcanaṃ hiṃhaṃ, dhi ha hi dha dhunā rahi.

The particles and suffixes are: tuna, tvāna, tave, tvā, tuṃ; and dhā, so, thā, kkhattuṃ, meva; and to, tha, tra, hiñcanaṃ, hiṃhaṃ; and dhi, ha, hi, dha, dhunā, rahi.

1203.

1203.

Dāni vodācanaṃ dājja, thaṃ tattaṃ jjha jju ādayo;

Samāso cā’byayībhāvo,

Yādeso cā’byayaṃ bhaveti.

Now, the explanation: the particles 'thaṃ', 'tattaṃ', 'jjha', 'jju', and so on; also the abyayībhāva compound, and that which is a substitute for it, become indeclinable.

Iti abyayavaggo.

Thus ends the chapter on indeclinables.

Sāmaññakaṇḍo tatiyo.

The Third, the General Section.

Abhidhānappadīpikā samattā.

The Abhidhānappadīpikā is completed.

Nigamana

Conclusion

1.

1.

Saggakaṇḍo [M 0.0099] ca bhūkaṇḍo, tathā sāmaññakaṇḍiti;

Kaṇḍattayānvitā esā, abhidhānappadīpikā.

The chapter on heaven, the chapter on earth, and likewise the chapter on general terms—this Abhidhānappadīpikā is furnished with three chapters.

2.

2.

Tidive mahiyaṃ bhujagāvasathe,

Sakalatthasamavhayadīpani’yaṃ;

Iha yo kusalo matimā sa naro,

Paṭu hoti mahāmunino vacane.

In heaven, on earth, and in the abode of serpents, this is the lamp named 'The Collection of All Meanings'; here, whatever man is skilled and intelligent becomes adept in the words of the Great Sage.

3.

3.

Parakkamabhujo nāma, bhūpālo guṇabhūsano;

Laṅkāya māsi tejassī, jayī kesarivikkamo.

There was in Laṅkā a king named Parakkamabhuja, an ornament of virtue, majestic, victorious, and with the prowess of a lion.

4.

4.

Vibhinnaṃ ciraṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ nikāya-

Ttayasmiñca kāresi sammā samagge;

Sadehaṃ’va niccādaro dīghakālaṃ,

Mahagghehi rakkhesi yo paccayehi.

He who rightly and thoroughly united the long-divided community of monks in the three Nikāyas; and with constant care for a long time, protected them with magnificent requisites as if they were his own body.

5.

5.

Yena laṅkā vihārehi, gāmā’rāmapurīhi ca;

Kittiyā viya sambādhī, katā khettehi vāpihi.

By whom Laṅkā was made to teem with monasteries, villages, parks, and cities, and with fields and reservoirs, as if with his own fame.

6.

6.

Yassā’sādhāraṇaṃ patvā, nuggahaṃ sabbakāmadaṃ;

Ahampi ganthakārattaṃ, patto vibudhagocaraṃ.

Having attained his incomparable patronage, which grants all desires, I too have attained the state of authorship, a sphere of the wise.

7.

7.

Kārite tena pāsāda, gopurādivibhūsite;

Saggakhaṇḍe’va tattoyā, sayasmiṃ patibimbite.

In the palace built by him, adorned with gate-towers and the like, which was like a fragment of heaven reflected in its own water,

8.

8.

Mahājetavanā’khyamhi, vihāre sādhusammate;

Sarogāmasamūhamhi, vasatā santavuttinā.

in the monastery named Mahājetavana, esteemed by the virtuous, while dwelling in the community of Sarogāma, by one of peaceful conduct.

9.

9.

Saddhammaṭṭhitikāmena, moggallānena dhīmatā;

Therena racitā esā, abhidhānappadīpikāti.

This Abhidhānappadīpikā was composed by the wise Elder Moggallāna, who desires the stability of the true Dhamma.
